The adversarial agent determined free_choice: stage 073 labels it "a reference-branch numerical freeze, not a new theorem," built from posited L/a≈1.85 + ε_r=1/20; r_F1 = √((12/π²)(37/20)²−1) is a deterministic function of it; benchmarks.yaml has NO entry referencing 37/20 / 1.778 / 1.85; the genuine external class is 54/5 (P0_target/N_Q → GR 2G/5c^5, which DOES carry benchmark IDs). My preliminary position: ratify **37/20 = free_choice, disclosure adequate** (no fatal flaw); the action is a non-conceptual provenance-label correction (r_F1 bundles published_target → free_choice) deferred to Step-6 close-out, surfaced to the user as the central-adjudication resolution. research/pde_ledger/notes/stages/moving_throat_pde_stage101_natural_source_map_reduction.md:13:This stage shows that in the strict point-particle limit the remaining reduced 2.5PN obstruction is no longer a mixed source/outgoing product. It is purely the outgoing-normalization factor `chi_Q`.
research/pde_ledger/notes/stages/moving_throat_pde_stage101_natural_source_map_reduction.md:29:`N_Q = 1/chi_Q`.
research/pde_ledger/notes/stages/moving_throat_pde_stage101_natural_source_map_reduction.md:33:- if `chi_Q = 1`, then `N_Q = 1`,
research/pde_ledger/notes/stages/moving_throat_pde_stage101_natural_source_map_reduction.md:34:- if `chi_Q != 1`, then the entire reduced 2.5PN mismatch is just the inverse outgoing renormalization.
research/pde_ledger/notes/stages/moving_throat_pde_stage101_natural_source_map_reduction.md:51:`chi_Q = 1`.
research/pde_ledger/notes/stages/moving_throat_pde_stage101_natural_source_map_reduction.md:55:`N_Q = 1`.
research/pde_ledger/notes/stages/moving_throat_pde_stage101_natural_source_map_reduction.md:65:`Delta_Q := chi_Q - 1`.

     1	# Moving-Throat PDE — Stage 121: Geometric Selection of the Core Hybridization Ratio
     2	
     3	## Goal
     4	
     5	Turn the normalized core-hybridization parameter
     6	\[
     7	\mathfrak r=\frac{\lambda}{\sqrt{K_sK_q}}
     8	\]
     9	from a free reduced number into an explicit branch value.
    10	
    11	## Parent motivation
    12	
    13	The parent 4D stack already keeps the throat geometry explicit through the collective throat variables `(a,L)` in the confinement / geometry sector, while the localized-Maxwell/plasma papers keep the mixed channels
    14	\[
    15	A_w,\qquad F_{\mu w},\qquad J^w
    16	\]
    17	in the microscopic ontology outside the strict far-field brane limit. fileciteturn33file4 fileciteturn33file1turn33file3
    18	The carried constructive hierarchy also keeps the preferred aspect ratio
    19	\[
    20	L/a \approx 1.85.
    21	\]
    22	fileciteturn35file0
    23	
    24	So the first concrete branch test is to identify the auxiliary mixed D/N tube of Stage 116 with the **actual throat axial span**:
    25	\[
    26	\boxed{L_W=L.}
    27	\]
    28	
    29	This is the simplest honest moving-throat core identification: the mixed side-channel is not a detached auxiliary cavity but the same axial throat corridor already selected by the carried geometry branch.
    30	
    31	## 1. Exact geometric branch law
    32	
    33	Stage 116 already fixed the compensation-selected D/N tube length to
    34	\[
    35	L_W=\frac{\pi a}{2}\sqrt{\frac{1+\mathfrak r^2}{3}}.
    36	\]
    37	Imposing \(L_W=L\) gives the exact geometric hybridization branch
    38	\[
    39	\boxed{
    40	\mathfrak r_{\rm geom}\!\left(\frac{L}{a}\right)
    41	=
    42	\sqrt{\frac{12}{\pi^2}\left(\frac{L}{a}\right)^2-1}.
    43	}
    44	\]
    45	
    46	So the normalized shell/mixed hybridization is no longer free once the mixed tube is identified with the actual throat length.
    47	
    48	The existence condition is
    49	\[
    50	\boxed{
    51	\frac{L}{a}\ge \frac{\pi}{2\sqrt3}\approx 0.9069,
    52	}
    53	\]
    54	which is comfortably satisfied on the carried throat branch.
    55	
    56	## 2. Family-1 / preferred-aspect-ratio value
    57	
    58	Using the carried preferred aspect ratio
    59	\[
    60	\frac{L}{a}=\frac{37}{20},
    61	\]
    62	the geometric branch value is
    63	\[
    64	\boxed{
    65	\mathfrak r_{F1}
    66	=
    67	\sqrt{\frac{12}{\pi^2}\left(\frac{37}{20}\right)^2-1}
    68	=
    69	\frac{\sqrt{4107-100\pi^2}}{10\pi}
    70	\approx 1.77799353547498.
    71	}
    72	\]
    73	
    74	Equivalently,
    75	\[
    76	\boxed{
    77	r_c^{F1}=\mathfrak r_{F1}^2\approx 3.16126101219081.
    78	}
    79	\]
    80	
    81	So the first concrete moving-throat core solve already fixes the static/mixed hybridization to an `O(1)` value rather than a tunable small parameter.
    82	
    83	## 3. Immediate mixed-tube consequence
    84	
    85	With \(L_W=L\), the auxiliary mixed-tube pole is simply
    86	\[
    87	\boxed{
    88	\Omega_W=\frac{\pi c_s}{2L},
    89	}
    90	\]
    91	the first D/N half-wave of the actual throat corridor. So the mixed side-channel is now tied directly to the real throat geometry, not to a detached bookkeeping cavity.
    92	
    93	## Result
    94	
    95	The surviving outlet-core problem has already shrunk:
    96	
    97	- before Stage 121, the normalized hybridization \(\mathfrak r\) was free;
    98	- after identifying the mixed D/N tube with the actual throat length, it is fixed to
    99	  \[
   100	  \mathfrak r_{F1}\approx 1.778
   101	  \]
   102	  on the preferred aspect-ratio branch.
   103	
   104	So the next question is no longer “what is \(\mathfrak r\)?” It is “what normalized mouth-coupling ratio \(\mathfrak g\) does the actual core pick at this geometrically fixed \(\mathfrak r\)?”

     1	# Moving-Throat PDE — Stage 194: Exact Outgoing `l=2` DtN Fingerprint, Fixing of `\chi_Q`, and the Isotropic Deformation Algebra
     2	
     3	## Status
     4	
     5	**Exact within the carried Stage 193 isotropic grouped-real `P2` conservative surface, the reduced 2.5PN outgoing compiler, and the canonical compact passive/outgoing `l=2` DtN closure already isolated in the 5PN notes.**
     6	
     7	This stage does **not** introduce a new constitutive law.
     8	It upgrades Stage 193 from the exact conservative isotropic one-pole front end to the first exact **retarded** isotropic branch test.
     9	
    10	---
    11	
    12	## Purpose
    13	
    14	Stage 193 froze the conservative grouped-real `P2` target surface
    15	\[
    16	 a_2=b_2=a_4=b_4=0,
    17	 \qquad
    18	 \Delta_{\rm pole}=\bar\nu_4-4\bar\nu_2^{\,2}=0,
    19	\]
    20	and therefore the exact one-parameter conservative carrier
    21	\[
    22	\widehat Y_Q^{\rm cons}(\omega)
    23	=
    24	\frac34+\frac14\frac{1}{1-\omega^2/\Omega_Q^2}.
    25	\]
    26	That was the right front end, but it still left one reduced scalar open:
    27	
    28	> once the grouped-real `P2` branch is isotropic and one-pole conservative, what fixes the **leading outgoing `l=2` normalization** on the actual passive/outgoing branch?
    29	
    30	The 5PN chain narrowed that remaining freedom to the single scalar
    31	\[
    32	\chi_Q,
    33	\]
    34	which multiplies the leading odd `\omega^5` term in the isotropic retarded grouped-`P2` module. This stage freezes the next audited theorem target:
    35	
    36	1. the exact outgoing spherical `l=2` Dirichlet-to-Neumann fingerprint,
    37	2. the exact fixing
    38	   \[
    39	   \chi_Q=1
    40	   \]
    41	   on the canonical compact passive/outgoing branch,
    42	3. and the exact first isotropic deformation algebra showing which DtN-side branch data can move `\chi_Q` while preserving the canonical even moments.
    43	
    44	So Stage 194 is the first precise retarded continuation of the Stage 193 conservative surface.
    45	
    46	---
    47	
    48	## 1. Carry-forward retarded grouped-`P2` module
    49	
    50	From Stage 193, the isotropic one-pole conservative carrier is already fixed through `O(\omega^4)`.
    51	The corresponding retarded grouped-`P2` one-pole-plus-contact module is written as
    52	\[
    53	\boxed{
    54	\widehat Y_Q^{\rm ret}(\omega)
    55	=
    56	\frac34
    57	+
    58	\frac14\,
    59	\frac{1}{1-\omega^2/\Omega_Q^2-i\chi_Q\,\sigma_Q^{\rm can}\,\omega^5}
    60	+O(\omega^6).
    61	}
    62	\]
    63	Here:
    64	
    65	- `\Omega_Q` is the Stage 193 conservative pole scale,
    66	- `\sigma_Q^{\rm can}` is the canonical compact outgoing normalization,
    67	- `\chi_Q` is the only reduced outgoing-normalization scalar that remains open before the explicit DtN model is inserted.
    68	
    69	Matching the even coefficients of Stage 193 to the canonical compact outgoing `l=2` branch fixes
    70	\[
    71	\boxed{
    72	\Omega_Q=\frac{3c_s}{2a},
    73	\qquad
    74	\sigma_Q^{\rm can}=\frac{9}{8\Omega_Q^5}=\frac{4a^5}{27c_s^5}.
    75	}
    76	\]
    77	Therefore the retarded module expands as
    78	\[
    79	\widehat Y_Q^{\rm ret}(\omega)
    80	=
    81	1+\frac{a^2\omega^2}{9c_s^2}
    82	+\frac{4a^4\omega^4}{81c_s^4}
    83	+i\,\chi_Q\,\frac{a^5\omega^5}{27c_s^5}
    84	+O(\omega^6).
    85	\]
    86	So the only leading retarded mismatch relative to the canonical compact branch is the multiplier `\chi_Q`.
    87	
    88	---
    89	
    90	## 2. Exact outgoing spherical `l=2` DtN fingerprint
    91	
    92	Define the dimensionless outgoing argument
    93	\[
    94	\boxed{z:=\frac{a\omega}{c_s}.}
    95	\]
    96	Let the canonical compact outgoing partial wave be the spherical Hankel mode
    97	\[
    98	h_2^{(1)}(z)=j_2(z)+i\,y_2(z).
    99	\]
   100	Then the exact outgoing `l=2` DtN operator is
   101	\[
   102	\boxed{
   103	\Lambda_2^{\rm out}(z)
   104	=
   105	 z\,\frac{d}{dz}\ln h_2^{(1)}(z)
   106	=
   107	 z\,\frac{h_2^{(1)\prime}(z)}{h_2^{(1)}(z)}.
   108	}
   109	\]
   110	Its small-`z` expansion is
   111	\[
   112	\boxed{
   113	\Lambda_2^{\rm out}(z)
   114	=
   115	-3+\frac{z^2}{3}+\frac{z^4}{9}
   116	+i\,\frac{z^5}{9}
   117	-\frac{2z^6}{27}
   118	-i\,\frac{z^7}{27}
   119	+O(z^8).
   120	}
   121	\]
   122	So the exact static slot is
   123	\[
   124	\Lambda_2^{\rm out}(0)=-3.
   125	\]
   126	
   127	Normalize by that static slot:
   128	\[
   129	\boxed{
   130	\widehat Y_2^{\rm out}(z)
   131	:=-\frac{3}{\Lambda_2^{\rm out}(z)}.
   132	}
   133	\]
   134	Then
   135	\[
   136	\boxed{
   137	\widehat Y_2^{\rm out}(z)
   138	=
   139	1+\frac{z^2}{9}
   140	+\frac{4z^4}{81}
   141	+i\,\frac{z^5}{27}
   142	-\frac{11z^6}{729}
   143	-i\frac{z^7}{243}
   144	+O(z^8).
   145	}
   146	\]
   147	Restoring `\omega`,
   148	\[
   149	\boxed{
   150	\widehat Y_2^{\rm out}(\omega)
   151	=
   152	1+\frac{a^2\omega^2}{9c_s^2}
   153	+\frac{4a^4\omega^4}{81c_s^4}
   154	+i\,\frac{a^5\omega^5}{27c_s^5}
   155	+O(\omega^6).
   156	}
   157	\]
   158	This is the exact canonical compact outgoing `l=2` fingerprint.
   159	
   160	---
   161	
   162	## 3. Exact fixing of `\chi_Q` on the canonical compact branch
   163	
   164	Compare the Stage 194 retarded grouped-`P2` module
   165	\[
   166	\widehat Y_Q^{\rm ret}(\omega)
   167	=
   168	1+\frac{a^2\omega^2}{9c_s^2}

   185	\]
   186	So on the canonical compact passive/outgoing `l=2` DtN branch, the last reduced outgoing-normalization scalar is fixed exactly.
   187	
   188	Equivalently, any deformed outgoing DtN branch of the form
   189	\[
   190	\Lambda_2^{\rm def}(z)
   191	=
   192	-3+\frac{z^2}{3}+\frac{z^4}{9}+i\,\xi_Q\,\frac{z^5}{9}+O(z^6)
   193	\]
   194	simply produces
   195	\[
   196	\boxed{\chi_Q=\xi_Q.}
   197	\]
   198	So after the canonical DtN model is inserted, the only reduced outgoing uncertainty left is deviation of the **actual** moving-throat isotropic branch from the exact compact outgoing fingerprint.
   199	
   200	---
   201

   203	
   204	To make that last statement explicit, take the first isotropic moving-throat DtN deformation family
   205	\[

   210	+\Sigma_0+\Sigma_2 z^2+\Sigma_4 z^4+i\Sigma_5 z^5+O(z^6).
   211	}
   212	\]
   213	Here:
   214	
   215	- `S` is an overall mouth normalization,
   216	- `\beta` rescales the effective outgoing argument,
   217	- `\Sigma_0,\Sigma_2,\Sigma_4` are isotropic even throat-core self-energy data,
   218	- `\Sigma_5` is the first extra isotropic odd `l=2` core outlet.

   227	\qquad
   228	L_2=\frac{S\beta^2}{3}+\Sigma_2,
   229	\qquad
   230	L_4=\frac{S\beta^4}{9}+\Sigma_4,

   106	\]
   107	
   108	So the finite Packet B of Stage 191 is not abstract. It is literally the quotient projection of the microscopic drift under `\(M_*\)`.
   109	
   110	The five free similarity coordinates are
   111	\[
   112	(\Delta_\lambda,\Delta_c,\Delta_\gamma,\Delta_U,\Delta_W),
   113	\]
   114	while the three dependent microscopic coordinates are
   115	\[
   116	(\Delta_T,\Delta_{K_\eta},\Delta_\mu).
   117	\]
   118	
   119	---
   120	
   121	## 2. Exact pivot block on the dependent triple
   122	
   123	Select the columns of `\(M_*\)` corresponding to the dependent triple in the order
   124	\[
   125	(T,K_\eta,\mu).
   126	\]
   127	Then
   128	\[
   129	\boxed{
   130	P_{(T,K_\eta,\mu)}:=M_*[:,(T,K_\eta,\mu)]
   131	=
   132	\begin{pmatrix}
   133	1+\chi_{0,*} & 0 & 0\\[4pt]
   134	-F_* & -1 & 1\\[4pt]
   135	0 & -1 & 0
   136	\end{pmatrix}.
   137	}
   138	\]
   139	Its determinant is
   140	\[
   141	\boxed{
   142	\det P_{(T,K_\eta,\mu)}=1+\chi_{0,*}>0.
   143	}
   144	\]
   145	So the dependent triple is an exact pivot block on the constructive coherent branch.
   146	
   147	The inverse is therefore exact:
   148	\[
   149	\boxed{
   150	P_{(T,K_\eta,\mu)}^{-1}
   151	=
   152	\begin{pmatrix}
   153	\dfrac{1}{1+\chi_{0,*}} & 0 & 0\\[8pt]
   154	0 & 0 & -1\\[4pt]
   155	\dfrac{F_*}{1+\chi_{0,*}} & 1 & -1
   156	\end{pmatrix}.
   157	}
   158	\]
   159	
   160	This already shows that the quotient coordinates can be reinserted into microscopic drift space using only the dependent triple.
   161	
   162	---
   163	
   164	## 3. Exact canonical quotient section
   165	
   166	Let
   167	\[
   168	E_{(T,K_\eta,\mu)}
   169	\]
   170	be the ambient `\(8\times 3\)` embedding that inserts a three-vector into the dependent positions
   171	\[
   172	(T,K_\eta,\mu)
   173	\]
   174	in the ordered drift basis `\((\lambda,c,\gamma,U,K_\eta,W,\mu,T)\)`.
   175	
   176	Define the canonical quotient section
   177	\[
   178	\boxed{
   179	S_{(T,K_\eta,\mu)}:=E_{(T,K_\eta,\mu)}\,P_{(T,K_\eta,\mu)}^{-1}.
   180	}
   181	\]
   182	Explicitly,
   183	\[
   184	\boxed{
   185	S_{(T,K_\eta,\mu)}=
   186	\begin{pmatrix}
   187	0 & 0 & 0\\
   188	0 & 0 & 0\\
   189	0 & 0 & 0\\
   190	0 & 0 & 0\\
   191	0 & 0 & -1\\
   192	0 & 0 & 0\\
   193	\dfrac{F_*}{1+\chi_{0,*}} & 1 & -1\\[8pt]
   194	\dfrac{1}{1+\chi_{0,*}} & 0 & 0
   195	\end{pmatrix}.
   196	}
   197	\]
   198	By construction,
   199	\[
   200	\boxed{M_*S_{(T,K_\eta,\mu)}=I_3.}
   201	\]
   202	So `\(S_{(T,K_\eta,\mu)}\)` is an exact right inverse of the quotient map.
   203	
   204	This means every quotient packet
   205	\[
   206	\mathbf q=(q_{\rm tr},q_{\rm nt},q_\eta)^T
   207	\]
   208	already has a canonical microscopic representative supported only on the dependent triple:
   209	\[
   210	\boxed{\Delta\mathbf x_{\rm quot}:=S_{(T,K_\eta,\mu)}\,\mathbf q.}
   211	\]
   212	
   213	---
   214	
   215	## 4. Exact complementary orbit/quotient projectors
   216	
   217	Define
   218	\[
   219	\boxed{Q_{\rm quot}:=S_{(T,K_\eta,\mu)}M_*,}
   220	qquad
   221	\boxed{O_{\rm orb}:=I_8-Q_{\rm quot}.}
   222	\]
   223	Then the projector identities are exact:
   224	\[
   225	\boxed{Q_{\rm quot}^2=Q_{\rm quot},}
   226	qquad
   227	\boxed{O_{\rm orb}^2=O_{\rm orb},}
   228	qquad
   229	\boxed{Q_{\rm quot}O_{\rm orb}=O_{\rm orb}Q_{\rm quot}=0.}
   230	\]
   231	Moreover,
   232	\[
   233	\boxed{M_*O_{\rm orb}=0,}
   234	qquad
   235	\boxed{M_*Q_{\rm quot}=M_*.}
   236	\]
   237	So for every microscopic drift,
   238	\[
   239	\boxed{
   240	\Delta\mathbf x
   241	=
   242	\underbrace{O_{\rm orb}\Delta\mathbf x}_{\Delta\mathbf x_{\rm orbit}}
   243	+
   244	\underbrace{Q_{\rm quot}\Delta\mathbf x}_{\Delta\mathbf x_{\rm fail}}.
   245	}
   246	\]
   247	This decomposition is exact and unique, with
   248	\[
   249	\boxed{\Delta\mathbf x_{\rm orbit}\in\ker M_*,}
   250	qquad
   251	\boxed{M_*\Delta\mathbf x_{\rm fail}=M_*\Delta\mathbf x=\mathbf q.}
   252	\]
   253	
   254	So `\(O_{\rm orb}\)` is the exact microscopic orbit projector, while `\(Q_{\rm quot}\)` is the exact quotient-failure projector.
   255	
   256	---
   257	
   258	## 5. Exact support of the quotient-failure piece
   259	
   260	Let
   261	\[
   262	\mathbf q=M_*\Delta\mathbf x=
   263	\begin{pmatrix}
   264	q_{\rm tr}\\
   265	q_{\rm nt}\\
   266	q_\eta
   267	\end{pmatrix}.
   268	\]
   269	Then the quotient-failure piece is
   270	\[
   271	\boxed{\Delta\mathbf x_{\rm fail}=Q_{\rm quot}\Delta\mathbf x=S_{(T,K_\eta,\mu)}\mathbf q.}
   272	\]
   273	So its only nonzero components are
   274	\[
   275	\boxed{(\Delta_T)_{\rm fail}=\frac{q_{\rm tr}}{1+\chi_{0,*}},}
   276	\]
   277	\[
   278	\boxed{(\Delta_{K_\eta})_{\rm fail}=-q_\eta,}
   279	\]
   280	\[
   281	\boxed{(\Delta_\mu)_{\rm fail}=\frac{F_*}{1+\chi_{0,*}}q_{\rm tr}+q_{\rm nt}-q_\eta.}
   282	\]
   283	All five free similarity directions vanish in `\(\Delta\mathbf x_{\rm fail}\)`.
   284	
   285	This is the sharpest microscopic result of the stage:
   286	
   287	> **the entire failure of single-orbit lock is carried only by the dependent triple**
   288	> \((\Delta_T,\Delta_{K_\eta},\Delta_\mu)\).
   289	
   290	So the five free similarity coordinates never directly carry quotient failure.
   291	
   292	---
   293	
   294	## 6. Exact orbit piece and the single-orbit law
   295	
   296	Because
   297	\[
   298	\Delta\mathbf x_{\rm orbit}=O_{\rm orb}\Delta\mathbf x,
   299	\]
   300	the orbit projector leaves the five free coordinates unchanged:
   301	\[
   302	(\Delta_\lambda,\Delta_c,\Delta_\gamma,\Delta_U,\Delta_W)_{\rm orbit}
   303	=
   304	(\Delta_\lambda,\Delta_c,\Delta_\gamma,\Delta_U,\Delta_W).
   305	\]
   306	It rewrites only the dependent triple, forcing it onto the exact Stage 187 single-orbit law.
   307	
   308	Define
   309	\[
   310	\boxed{\alpha_*:=\frac{1+\delta_{U,*}}{1+\chi_{0,*}}.}
   311	\]
   312	Then the orbit piece satisfies
   313	\[
   314	\boxed{(\Delta_{K_\eta})_{\rm orbit}=2\Delta_c-\Delta_U,}
   315	\]
   316	\[
   317	\boxed{(\Delta_T)_{\rm orbit}=\Delta_U-\alpha_*(\Delta_\gamma+\Delta_c-\Delta_U),}
   318	\]
   319	\[
   320	\boxed{
   321	(\Delta_\mu)_{\rm orbit}
   322	=
   323	2\Delta_c-\Delta_U+2\Delta_W-2\Delta_\lambda
   324	-
   325	E_*(2\Delta_\gamma+2\Delta_\lambda-\Delta_U-\Delta_W)
   326	-
   327	F_*\alpha_*(\Delta_\gamma+\Delta_c-\Delta_U).
   328	}
   329	\]
   330	These are exactly the Stage 187 finite orbit formulas recovered by projection.
   331	
   332	So the projector calculus does not invent a new orbit law.
   333	It isolates the exact same law as the unique orbit representative with the same five free coordinates as the original microscopic drift.
   334	
   335	---
   336	
   337	## 7. Exact orbit-lock theorem in projector form
   338	
   339	The exact orbit-lock condition now has several equivalent forms:
   340	\[
   341	\boxed{\mathbf q=0,}
   342	\qquad
   343	\boxed{M_*\Delta\mathbf x=0,}
   344	\qquad
   345	\boxed{Q_{\rm quot}\Delta\mathbf x=0,}
   346	\qquad
   347	\boxed{\Delta\mathbf x=O_{\rm orb}\Delta\mathbf x,}
   348	\qquad
   349	\boxed{\Delta\mathbf x\in\ker M_*.}
   350	\]
   351	So the Packet-B zero-set of Stage 191 is now a literal microscopic projector statement.
   352	
   353	In words:
   354	
   355	- Stage 191 said the reduced orbit packet must vanish.
   356	- Stage 192 shows that this is exactly the statement that the candidate microscopic drift has no quotient-supported dependent-triple correction.
   357	
   358	That is the clean bridge from the finite-packet theorem back to the actual moving-throat PDE branch.
   359	
   360	---
   361	
   362	## 8. What this stage fixes, and what remains for Stage 193
   363	
   364	### Fixed here
   365	
   366	This stage fixes the exact microscopic geometry of the orbit packet.
   367	In particular:
   368	
   369	1. Packet B is exactly the quotient projection `\(M_*\Delta\mathbf x\)`.
   370	2. The orbit/failure split is exact and complementary.
   371	3. The quotient failure lives only on the dependent triple.
   372	4. The orbit projector leaves the five similarity coordinates untouched and restores the exact Stage 187 single-orbit dependent law.
   373	
   374	### Still missing
   375	
   376	What this stage does **not** yet do is compose the microscopic projector with the observable defect packet
   377	\[
   378	(\Theta_1,\Xi_1,\mathcal R_1).
   379	\]
   380	That is the next step.
   381	
   382	Stage 193 will take the exact observable inversion from the earlier compiler stages and feed it through the canonical quotient section
   383	\[
   384	S_{(T,K_\eta,\mu)}
   385	\]
   386	to obtain the exact observable-to-microscopic correction compiler.
   387	
   388	---
   389	
   390	## 9. Script-backed status
   391	
   392	The accompanying SymPy audit verifies:
   393	
   394	- the exact Stage 187 monomial-drift matrix `\(M_*\)`,
   395	- the dependent pivot block `\(P_{(T,K_\eta,\mu)}\)` and its inverse,
   396	- the exact right-inverse section `\(S_{(T,K_\eta,\mu)}\)`,
   397	- the complementary projector identities for `\(Q_{\rm quot}\)` and `\(O_{\rm orb}\)`,
   398	- the support of the quotient-failure projector only on the dependent triple,
   399	- the exact formulas for the failure components in terms of `\((q_{\rm tr},q_{\rm nt},q_\eta)\)`,
   400	- the exact orbit-law formulas for the projected dependent coordinates,
   401	- and the equivalence
   402	  \[
   403	  Q_{\rm quot}\Delta\mathbf x=0
   404	  \iff
   405	  M_*\Delta\mathbf x=0.
   406	  \]
   407	
   408	Supporting files:
   409	
   410	- `moving_throat_pde_stage192_orbit_quotient_projectors_sympy_audit.py`
   411	- `moving_throat_pde_stage192_orbit_quotient_projectors_sympy_audit_output.txt`

**Item 1 — `fit_stage192_chi_q_unit_relations`**

DEFENSE verdict: **PARTIAL**. The strongest defense is that [stage_192.tex](/var/projects/toy_physics/research/pde_ledger/paper/stages/stage_192.tex:17) says the card is preserving a block narrative, not giving a second proof, and the `Delta_Q/chi_Q/N_Q` language is true later in the Packet-A finish-line chain. But as a literal stage-192 card, it overclaims: [stage_192.tex](/var/projects/toy_physics/research/pde_ledger/paper/stages/stage_192.tex:13) says Stage 192 collapses Packet A to `Delta_Q=chi_Q-1`, and lines 20-21 check use of `Delta_Q` and `N_Q=chi_Q^{-1}`. The stage-192 note itself is only orbit/quotient projector calculus, with `chi_{0,*}` in the matrices and a narrow output: quotient failure support and orbit projection. It also explicitly says it does **not** yet compose the projector with the observable defect packet at [stage192 note](/var/projects/toy_physics/research/pde_ledger/notes/stages/moving_throat_pde_stage192_orbit_quotient_projectors.md:376).

Stage 194 is where `chi_Q=1` is fixed, at [stage194 note](/var/projects/toy_physics/research/pde_ledger/notes/stages/moving_throat_pde_stage194_outgoing_l2_fingerprint_and_deformation_algebra.md:162) and [line 184](/var/projects/toy_physics/research/pde_ledger/notes/stages/moving_throat_pde_stage194_outgoing_l2_fingerprint_and_deformation_algebra.md:184). Stage 195 is where `Delta_Q := chi_Q - 1` and `N_Q=chi_Q^{-1}` are established, at [stage195 note](/var/projects/toy_physics/research/pde_ledger/notes/stages/moving_throat_pde_stage195_source_map_reduction_of_canonical_outgoing_branch.md:185) and [line 215](/var/projects/toy_physics/research/pde_ledger/notes/stages/moving_throat_pde_stage195_source_map_reduction_of_canonical_outgoing_branch.md:215).

ADJUDICATION verdict: **PARTIAL**. The card-text finding stands, but only as a close-out presentation/provenance fix. It is not fatal to the derivation cone, because the claimed identities are supported downstream at 194/195; they are just misattributed to 192.

**Item 2 — `chain_aspect_37_20`**

DEFENSE verdict: **PARTIAL**. The substantive program is defensible: 37/20 is disclosed as a free/reference branch choice, not a published target. Stage 073 introduces `L/a = 37/20` and immediately says it is a “reference-branch numerical freeze, not a new theorem” at [stage073 note](/var/projects/toy_physics/research/pde_ledger/notes/stages/moving_throat_pde_stage073_family1_geometry_map.md:54). Stage 121 then derives `r_F1` deterministically from that carried value via `sqrt((12/pi^2)(37/20)^2 - 1)` at [stage121 note](/var/projects/toy_physics/research/pde_ledger/notes/stages/moving_throat_pde_stage121_geometric_r_selection.md:58). `benchmarks.yaml` has GR/P0 benchmark entries, but no 37/20, 1.85, or 1.778 benchmark entry.

I concede the downstream provenance labels are stale in places: [fit_stage156...](/var/projects/toy_physics/research/pde_ledger/redteam_adversarial/provenance/fit_stage156_carried_constants_block__r_f1.yaml:191), [fit_stage158...](/var/projects/toy_physics/research/pde_ledger/redteam_adversarial/provenance/fit_stage158_canonical_quartet_literals__r_f1.yaml:193), and [fit_stage161...](/var/projects/toy_physics/research/pde_ledger/redteam_adversarial/provenance/fit_stage161_rf1_family1_radius__r_f1.yaml:43) label `r_F1` as `published_target`. That should be `free_choice`, inherited from the 37/20 branch freeze. Later bundles already reflect that: [fit_stage162...](/var/projects/toy_physics/research/pde_ledger/redteam_adversarial/provenance/fit_stage162_rf1_family1_radius__r_f1.yaml:43) and [fit_stage163...](/var/projects/toy_physics/research/pde_ledger/redteam_adversarial/provenance/fit_stage163_family1_canonical_point_block__r_f1.yaml:130) say `free_choice`. The `fit_stage163_Rstar_quarter` record is also correctly flagged as a scanner mislabel: its own finding says `1/4` is `R_q/R_*`, not `r_F1`, at [fit_stage163_Rstar_quarter](/var/projects/toy_physics/research/pde_ledger/redteam_adversarial/provenance/fit_stage163_rstar_quarter__r_f1.yaml:41).

ADJUDICATION verdict: **FIND_STANDS** on the classification correction: `L/a = 37/20` and derived `r_F1` are `free_choice`, not `published_target`. This is non-fatal and non-conceptual: provenance-label cleanup, not a derivation failure.

Overall: I concur that batch-1 leaves only close-out fixes: stage-192 card text, plus provenance-label corrections for `r_F1` and the already-evidenced `V_known`/`Pe` anchor cleanup; no fatal flaw survives.
